Lomanov emphasizes that the SCO&\#x27;s &\#x27;Shanghai Spirit&\#x27;—based on mutual trust, benefit, equality, consultation, respect for diverse civilizations, and common development—provides a solid foundation for its growth. The article highlights President Xi Jinping&\#x27;s &\#x27;four persistences&\#x27; \(prioritizing development, security, people-centric approaches, and dialogue\) as key guidelines for the organization&\#x27;s future, aiming to deepen cooperation in areas like trade, investment, and artificial intelligence while promoting a more just global governance system.

rss · 人民日报\(People&\#x27;s Daily\) · Oct 8, 00:26

**「Background on SCO」** The Shanghai Cooperation Organisation is a permanent intergovernmental organization founded in 2001, known for being the world&\#x27;s largest by area and population among regional cooperation organizations. It focuses on enhancing political, economic, and security cooperation among its member states, primarily in Central Asia and surrounding regions, under the framework of the &\#x27;Shanghai Spirit&\#x27; which rejects military alliances and confrontation.

**「Impact」** This diplomatic discourse reinforces the SCO&\#x27;s role as a significant platform for Eurasian cooperation and global south engagement, potentially influencing regional trade and security policies. The emphasis on AI and tech cooperation signals a strategic direction for member states to leverage technology for economic development.

<a id="item-tech-news-71"></a>
### [Abercrombie painting resold for $1.35 million after $30 purchase](https://www.faz.net/aktuell/feuilleton/kunstmarkt/gertrude-abercrombie-in-altem-hausrat-gefundenes-gemaelde-fuer-1-35-millionen-dollar-versteigert-201296554.html) ⭐️ 0.0/10

A painting by the American Surrealist Gertrude Abercrombie, originally bought privately for about thirty dollars, was sold at auction in New York for $1.35 million. The result highlights strong market demand for works by long-overlooked Surrealist women artists. The transaction underscores how overlooked artworks can appreciate significantly when rediscovered and brought to auction.

rss · 法兰克福汇报\(Frankfurter Allgemeine Zeitung \(FAZ\)\) · Oct 7, 13:35

**「Background」** Gertrude Abercrombie \(1907–1977\) was an American painter associated with Surrealism, known for dreamlike imagery often centered on horses, domestic interiors, and self-portraiture. Her work gained greater recognition decades after her death, as art markets and institutions have increasingly revisited contributions by women artists who were historically underrepresented.

**「Impact」** The auction result demonstrates continued growth in collector interest for mid-century Surrealist works by women, which can materially benefit owners of overlooked artworks and affect pricing trends in that niche segment. It also signals that provenance and prior undervaluation can play a major role in sudden revaluations at auction.

**Tags**: `#art market`, `#auction`, `#surrealism`

<a id="item-tech-news-88"></a>
### [China reports 21.44 billion cross-regional trips during National Day holiday](http://paper.people.com.cn/rmrb/pc/content/202610/08/content_30184434.html) ⭐️ ?/10

According to a People&\#x27;s Daily report dated October 8, 2026, the Ministry of Transport estimates that total cross-regional personnel flows in China reached 2.144 billion person-times during the 2026 National Day holiday from October 1 to 7, averaging 306 million daily. Road travel remained the dominant mode, accounting for an estimated 196.42 billion person-times at an average of 28.06 million daily, while commercial road passenger volume grew 6.8% year-on-year to 286.76 million. Rail客运 volume surged 13.1% to 152.36 million, water transport rose 7.8% to 10.99 million, and civil aviation remained essentially flat year-on-year at 16.95 million person-times.

rss · 人民日报\(People&\#x27;s Daily\) · Oct 8, 00:26

**「Background」** The National Day holiday, also known as Golden Week, is China&\#x27;s longest annual travel period, typically generating massive cross-regional mobility as workers and students return home or tour domestically. The Ministry of Transport publishes pre- and post-holiday flow estimates to help plan capacity and inform the public.

**「Impact」** Transport operators and infrastructure managers should expect sustained high rail and road demand during peak holiday windows, with rail growth outpacing other modes. No immediate policy changes are indicated, but the data may influence future capacity investments in high-speed rail and highway corridors.

**「Community Discussion」** No community comments are available for this item.

<a id="item-tech-news-110"></a>
### [Wangjiang County builds billion-yuan childrenswear industry](http://paper.people.com.cn/rmrb/pc/content/202610/08/content_30184473.html) ⭐️ ?/10

Anchoring its historical cotton-growing base, Wangjiang County in Anqing, Anhui Province, has transformed into a major textile hub with over 6,900 production entities and industry revenue reaching 22.5 billion yuan in 2025, up 24%. The county now hosts more than 3,000 childrenswear firms, creating a specialized cluster recognized by China&\#x27;s Ministry of Industry and Information Technology under its regional development initiatives. Manufacturers are investing heavily in automation and AI quality inspection to cut energy use by 20% while boosting output by 5,000万件 annually. Meanwhile, the county&\#x27;s e-commerce sector processed over 1 billion childrenswear parcels in the first seven months of 2026, driven by 800+ live-streaming businesses and next-day delivery to the Yangtze River Delta region.

rss · 人民日报\(People&\#x27;s Daily\) · Oct 8, 00:26

**「Background」** Wangjiang County has a long tradition of cotton cultivation dating back centuries, with peak planting areas exceeding 400,000 mu. The local government identified textiles as a strategic pillar industry in 2010, capitalizing on coastal manufacturing relocation and the rise of e-commerce to build a complete supply chain from raw materials to finished goods distribution.

**「Impact」** The county&\#x27;s 150,000+ textile workers and 5,700+ e-commerce entities now benefit from integrated manufacturing-logistics capabilities that enable same-day production-to-shipment cycles for bulk orders. The recognition as a national key培育 district provides access to targeted policy support for further industrial upgrading in central and western China.

---

<a id="item-tech-news-128"></a>
### [China issues first five-year national plan for disease control](http://paper.people.com.cn/rmrb/pc/content/202610/08/content_30184497.html) ⭐️ ?/10

On October 8, 2026, the State Disease Control and Prevention Administration and six other departments issued the Disease Control &\#x27;15th Five-Year Plan,&\#x27; the first five-year national special plan in China&\#x27;s disease control sector. Published in People&\#x27;s Daily, the plan outlines a strategy to basically build a modern disease control system with Chinese characteristics by 2030. It aims to transform governance from professional prevention to systematic social co-governance, integrate multi-disease prevention strategies, strengthen early detection and rapid response capabilities for emerging infectious diseases, and shift the focus toward preventive care through expanded vaccination services and medical-prevention integration.

rss · 人民日报\(People&\#x27;s Daily\) · Oct 8, 00:26

**「Context」** The &\#x27;15th Five-Year Plan&\#x27; period \(2026-2030\) builds upon the &\#x27;14th Five-Year Plan&\#x27; period, during which China underwent a historic systemic restructuring of its disease control system following the COVID-19 pandemic. Key achievements during this prior period included the completion of disease control institution reforms, expansion of infectious disease monitoring to over 84,000 medical institutions, construction of biosafety level-3 laboratories in 29 provinces, and the inclusion of HPV vaccines in the national immunization program.

**「Impact」** The plan establishes binding targets for China&\#x27;s public health infrastructure, including building a ready-plus-reserve epidemiological investigation workforce of approximately 77,000 personnel and developing a smart, multi-point trigger surveillance and early warning system. It requires all secondary and above healthcare institutions to provide vaccination services and pilots the authorization of public health physicians to issue vaccine prescriptions, directly affecting healthcare delivery standards nationwide.

---

<a id="item-tech-news-132"></a>
### [China Aviation Association calls for cabin crew protection](http://paper.people.com.cn/rmrb/pc/content/202610/08/content_30184501.html) ⭐️ ?/10

Following an October 2026 incident in which a China Eastern Airlines passenger verbally attacked and threatened flight attendants after a minor cart contact, the China Association of Civil Aviation issued a statement on 5 October affirming that it firmly supports member airlines&\#x27; efforts to legally protect employees&\#x27; rights during duties. The airline completed evidence collection and formally reported the incident to police, while also providing psychological counseling to the involved crew members. The association called on carriers to optimize in-flight disturbance handling procedures and urged passengers to use legal channels such as complaints or litigation rather than abusive conduct. This reporting appeared in People&\#x27;s Daily on 8 October.

rss · 人民日报\(People&\#x27;s Daily\) · Oct 8, 00:26

**「Background」** Airline cabin crew can be exposed to disruptive passenger behavior, and organizations such as the China Association of Civil Aviation often issue policy guidance or public statements when incidents raise safety or labor-rights concerns. In China, carriers and crew may pursue administrative or criminal remedies through public security and judicial channels for harassment or threats onboard.

**「Impact」** The association&\#x27;s statement signals stronger institutional backing for Chinese airlines and frontline crew facing abusive passengers, potentially influencing internal company protocols and passenger expectations. Practical effects will depend on how carriers implement new handling processes and whether authorities pursue further legal accountability in similar cases.

---

<a id="item-tech-news-134"></a>
### [Beijing police officer gives sweater to child at Tiananquan](http://paper.people.com.cn/rmrb/pc/content/202610/08/content_30184503.html) ⭐️ ?/10

On the early morning of October 4, 2026, during a national flag-raising ceremony at Tiananmen Square, Officer Wang Xiaoxuan from the Yongledian Police Station in Tongzhou District, Beijing, gave her sweater to a child from a Guangdong study group who was shivering in 8-degree Celsius weather. Wang continued her duty without hesitation, remaining in her thin police uniform, and later received a formal salute from the grateful child before returning her sweater. The incident, captured by onlookers, drew widespread praise online for demonstrating the bond between police and citizens, highlighting Wang&\#x27;s fifth year of service and her record of one third-class merit and two personal commendations.

rss · 人民日报\(People&\#x27;s Daily\) · Oct 8, 00:26

**「Background」** Tiananmen Square in Beijing hosts daily flag-raising ceremonies that attract large crowds of visitors, often requiring additional police support during peak times like National Day holiday periods in early October. Chinese police officers frequently engage in community service and public assistance as part of their duties, reflecting the official emphasis on &\#x27;serving the people&\#x27; as a core principle of the public security apparatus.

**「Impact」** This incident reinforces positive public perception of grassroots police officers in China and may encourage similar spontaneous acts of public service during crowded events. The story&\#x27;s publication in People&\#x27;s Daily on October 8, 2026, amplifies its reach as an example of ideal citizen-police relations.

---

<a id="item-tech-news-152"></a>
### [Xi Jinping Cultural Thought Drives China&\#x27;s Cultural Strength Push](https://www.qstheory.cn/20261008/40e52bde45ff4d96b2d14a9356c9577b/c.html) ⭐️ ?/10

An article published on October 8, 2026, reports that the &quot;15th Five-Year Plan&quot; starting year has seen solid progress in building a culturally strong nation under Xi Jinping Cultural Thought, which was formally proposed in October 2023. Key developments include the approval of Jingdezhen&\#x27;s ceramic heritage as China&\#x27;s 61st UNESCO World Heritage site in July 2026, the publication of selected works and theoretical research, and the integration of socialist core values into daily life and law. Cultural innovation and public services expanded significantly, with 3,253 public libraries, 7,188 museums, and nearly 44,000 community cultural institutions nationwide supporting growing domestic demand and international outreach through initiatives like the Global Civilization Initiative.

rss · 求是杂志\(Qiushi Journal\) · Oct 8, 00:48

**「Background」** Xi Jinping Cultural Thought is presented as a theoretical总结 of the Communist Party of China&\#x27;s cultural construction practices since the 19th National Congress, emphasizing the &\#x27;two integrations&\#x27;—combining Marxism with China&\#x27;s realities and with traditional Chinese culture. It serves as the guiding framework for propaganda, ideological work, and cultural policy in the new era.

**「Impact」** The policies have directly increased public access to cultural resources and boosted cultural tourism, with 600 million annual visitors to revolutionary sites and museums, and over 180,000 attendees at the 7th National Ethnic Arts Festival in 2026. Cultural industries are now formally recognized as economic drivers, contributing to employment through 14,900 intangible cultural heritage workshops supporting over 1.3 million people.

**「Community Discussion」** No community comments are available for this article.

<a id="item-tech-blog-2"></a>
### [Döblin Anticipated Rosa&\#x27;s Resonance Theory](https://www.faz.net/aktuell/feuilleton/debatten/alfred-doeblin-nahm-hartmut-rosas-theorie-der-resonanz-vorweg-201133381.html) ⭐️ 1.0/10

rss · 法兰克福汇报\(Frankfurter Allgemeine Zeitung \(FAZ\)\) · Oct 7, 18:30

**「Background」** Hartmut Rosa&\#x27;s contemporary resonance theory has gained significant traction, yet the conceptual groundwork appears to have been laid earlier by author Alfred Döblin in 1933.

**「Solution」** According to Helmuth Kiesel, Döblin effectively anticipated Rosa’s definitions of resonance prior to the Nazi regime&\#x27;s suppression of intellectual discourse. The historical catalyst that prevented Döblin&\#x27;s early theoretical contributions from influencing later academic thought was the book burnings carried out by the Nazis, which disrupted the continuity of such literary and philosophical developments.

**「Takeaway」** Döblin&\#x27;s 1933 work suggests that key elements of modern resonance theory were already present in literary thought before political interference obscured them.

**Tags**: `#literature`, `#resonance theory`, `#German culture`, `#book burning`, `#historical theory`

<a id="item-tech-blog-5"></a>
### [Organizations as Unreasonable Boyfriend](https://www.lesswrong.com/posts/ddpGW44q2vG9KakRT/orgs-unreasonable-boyfriend-as-service) ⭐️ ?/10

rss · LessWrong AI风险社区 · Oct 7, 21:35

**「Background」** Thomas Schelling observed that constraints can be advantageous in negotiation: an individual with limited resources or a binding commitment can secure better outcomes because their opponent must accept the constraint as real. Katja Grace extends this insight to explain why organizational membership often makes boundary-setting easier for individuals.

**「Solution」** Grace uses the metaphor of an unreasonable boyfriend to describe how organizations function as external constraints. A solo negotiator making a deadline faces skepticism because they could simply ignore it; the commitment is seen as self-negotiable. In contrast, an employee pointing to an organizational deadline is perceived as having their options legitimately cut off. This allows the individual to win negotiations for time and focus without appearing stubborn or unfriendly, as the organization acts as a third party that credibly enforces the constraint.

**「Takeaway」** Organizations provide a service by acting as an &\#x27;unreasonable boyfriend,&\#x27; credibly enforcing boundaries that solo individuals struggle to defend. This dynamic makes joining an org strategically attractive, as it trivializes the skill-intensive work of self-policing.

---

<a id="item-tech-blog-6"></a>
### [China&\#x27;s Water Project in Togo](http://paper.people.com.cn/rmrb/pc/content/202610/08/content_30184441.html) ⭐️ ?/10

rss · 人民日报\(People&\#x27;s Daily\) · Oct 8, 00:26

**「Background」** Rural villages in Togo&\#x27;s Plateaux region have long suffered from inadequate water infrastructure, forcing residents to trek kilometers daily to unsafe river sources, especially during dry seasons. This created serious health risks and placed a heavy burden on local communities, particularly women and children.

**「Solution」** China Aid to Togo Rural Water Project, constructed by Jiangxi China Coal Construction Group starting November 2024 and delivered in September 2026, addressed these challenges through 305 drilled wells serving over 300 villages and more than 170,000 people. The team faced significant obstacles: scattered villages accessible only by dirt roads, difficult transport of heavy equipment, high temperatures, and malaria risks. Geologically, the region&\#x27;s bedrock formations meant aquifers lay deeper than 60 meters, making traditional single-point drilling prone to producing dry wells. To overcome this, Chinese engineers employed multiple geophysical survey methods—natural field detection, electrical soundings, and transient electromagnetic techniques—to precisely map subsurface structures and identify water-rich zones before drilling. Beyond infrastructure, the project included capacity building: local workers received systematic training in drilling operations, equipment maintenance, and troubleshooting. One trained operator, Yawo, now handles both machine operation and site selection decisions. Local maintenance personnel were also trained to ensure long-term functionality after handover.

**「Takeaway」** The project demonstrates how targeted infrastructure investment combined with technical innovation and local capacity building can deliver sustainable water access in challenging environments, while strengthening bilateral relations through practical cooperation.

---

<a id="item-tech-blog-8"></a>
### [Midlife Crisis: Too Old for the City?](https://www.faz.net/aktuell/besser-leben/psychologie/ist-man-irgendwann-zu-alt-fuer-das-leben-in-der-grossstadt-accg-201291709.html) ⭐️ ?/10

rss · 法兰克福汇报\(Frankfurter Allgemeine Zeitung \(FAZ\)\) · Oct 7, 03:33

**「Background」** Stefanie de Velasco reflects on whether city life remains suitable as one ages, given her two decades in Berlin—a city she acknowledges is neither particularly beautiful nor clean. This tension between urban habits and the appeal of rural tranquility motivates her experiment.

**「Solution」** De Velasco attempts to transition to country living to escape urban drawbacks, but finds she cannot sustain it against the realities of rural life. Her narrative illustrates a personal reconciliation with the city, suggesting that aging does not necessarily require leaving urban environments behind despite their imperfections.

**「Takeaway」** Growing older does not mandate abandoning city life; personal adaptation matters more than geographical change.
